5. **CI (proposal for you to add; I do not touch `.github/`):** no GitHub Actions workflow builds or runs `Firmware/Tests/` (P0 finding; the README now says so). Minimal job, from the final review FR1:
   - file `.github/workflows/host-tests.yml`, triggered on push and pull request to `master` and `development`, `runs-on: ubuntu-latest`;
   - steps: `actions/checkout`, then `git submodule update --init --depth 1 Firmware/Libraries/CMSIS-DSP` (not `submodules: true`, which would also pull the Configurator and Hardware submodules);
   - matrix `opts: ["", "-DFFB_TESTS_USE_DSP=OFF", "-DFFB_TESTS_SANITIZE=ON"]`, each running `cmake -S Firmware/Tests -B build ${{ matrix.opts }} && cmake --build build -j && ctest --test-dir build --output-on-failure` (ctest already applies the documented exclusions and the baseline check);
   - MinGW as a separate optional job later: unverified (mallinfo shim, `VirtualAlloc` at 0x08000000, weak symbols).
   - Caveat: the runner's GCC (13) is untested here; no C++23 or GCC-14-only library use was found, so the risk is low.
   - Addition (P18, 2026-11-11): a 32-bit job is the same job with `-DFFB_TESTS_32BIT=ON` and a step `sudo apt-get install -y gcc-multilib g++-multilib` before configure (Ubuntu runners have no 32-bit libraries by default). Not verified on a runner.

The executable now gives 646/0/4 on GCC 16 (san 643/0/7, nodsp-san 645/0/5); the 316/2 baseline subset is checked by `check_baseline.py`. These are your files: shall I update the wording?

9. **TMC4671 Q1 (driver track D1):** the driver uses registers 0x1E, 0x47 and 0x5C, which datasheet Rev 2.07 does not document, and writes 0x41, which Rev 2.07 lists as read-only (bug 160). Was the driver written against an older register map you can supply?
10. **TMC4671 Q2:** the engineering-sample version value (SI_VERSION 0x00010000) is not in the datasheet; tested as characterization only.
11. **TMC4671 Q3:** the board init (`main.c:1377`, CubeMX) drives SPI1_SS2/SS3 low and nothing releases them before the first TMC_1 frame (NEEDS REVIEW 174). Intended?
12. **TMC4671 Q4:** the biquad enable bit (bug 162 = B-T24). DS §7.2 says bit 31 of CONFIG_DATA, the driver writes bit 0. Is there evidence from real hardware that the flux/torque filters are active (which would mean the datasheet is wrong)?

13. **ODrive D2-Q1:** the axis state, control mode, input mode numbers and error bits are not in the supplied 0.5.6 spec (PDF + CSV). Supply the 0.5.6 enums (`enums.py` or the API reference page) to turn those characterization assertions into spec checks.
14. **ODrive D2-Q2:** the driver comment names firmware 0.5.4, the spec is 0.5.6. Any differences for the messages the driver uses (0x01, 0x07, 0x09, 0x0B, 0x0E, 0x10, 0x17)?
15. **VESC D3-Q1:** spec gaps of the VESC slides: answered 2026-11-04 by the VESC firmware source `Tests/Code_Coverage/shared/specs/bldc-master` (7.01).
16. **D3-Q2 VESC (remaining after the source).** The supplied VESC source is 7.01. (a) In firmware 5.3 (the oldest version the driver accepts), did the FW_VERSION reply already have the `pairing_done` byte before the test-version byte? If not, bug B-V6 only affects newer VESC firmware. (b) Should unsolicited VESC STATUS frames count as a sign of life (the driver counts any received frame)?
17. **P11 G1-Q1 sqrt q15 accuracy (V41).** `arm_sqrt_q15` is up to 7 LSB from the rounded double sqrt at 6 of 32767 inputs (e.g. x=30840 gives 31796, reference 31789); ARM's own bound is 6 LSB and its pattern does not contain these inputs. Is ARM's 6 LSB the contract (then V41 becomes a NEEDS REVIEW tolerance item and the test bound is widened to a derived 7.2), or is the full-range accuracy a defect (the KNOWN_ISSUE test stays)? Until you decide, the KNOWN_ISSUE test stays.
18. **P11 q31 narrowing: wrap or saturate (NEEDS REVIEW 229).** In conv, correlate, partial conv, FIR, decimate and interpolate q31 the DOC says both "wraps around rather than clip" (about the 64-bit accumulator) and "saturated to 1.31 format" (about the final narrowing). For (-2^31)*(-2^31) the accumulator is 2^62 (no overflow) and the code returns 0x80000000 where saturation gives 0x7FFFFFFF. Today these are `characterization:` tests (G4 and G5 rule: contradictory DOC is never a plain test or a KNOWN_ISSUE). Reviewers argued that only the "saturated" sentence applies, i.e. an unambiguous DOC violation. Should they become `KNOWN_ISSUE` tests (intended: saturate)?
19. **P11 CLAUDE.md tier C wording.** CLAUDE.md says tier C is "only the CMSIS-DSP functions the firmware calls", but P11 (G1-G5) now tests every function of the modules the firmware uses (about 258 in-scope source files, 256 linked and measured). Shall I update the tier C line, and the TEST_PLAN.md wording, to say so? These are your files.
20. **Suspected firmware bug 39** (a critical error calls `emergencyStop(true)`, and `true` means reset). Left open at your instruction; say when you want it decided or tested differently.
21. **P11 correlate / conv padding (NEEDS REVIEW 228).** The DOC gives the output length 2*max-1 (the f32 DOC says to zero `pDst`) but the code never writes the padding outputs; ARM's tests pass only because their output buffer is pre-zeroed. Is this a defect (the code comments say zeros are included in the output) or acceptable? Today it is a `characterization:` test.
22. **P11 target path (`ARM_MATH_DSP`) (NEEDS REVIEW 231, 237).** The host builds the generic loops of the conv / correlate family, not the unrolled Cortex-M4 kernels; reviewer scratch builds with the DSP branch forced show extra failures (V71, V75, V76) that no test in the suite can see, and the host emulation of the SIMD intrinsics is itself unfaithful (V74, V77). Should I propose a second suite configuration with the DSP branch (a CMake change, not made), and one sanitizer configuration without `-fno-sanitize-recover` or with `no_sanitize` on the DSP objects, so that UB-hidden kernels get visible coverage?
23. **P11 host-emulation findings (V74, V77).** About 190 UB-hidden cases and V74 come from the portable emulation of SIMD intrinsics (`dsp/none.h`, `arm_math_memory.h`), which the Cortex-M4 firmware does not use (it has the real instructions). Shall they stay listed as vendor bugs (current state), or move to a separate "host-emulation only" list that does not count in the vendor bug total?

29. ~~**P14-P17 CLAUDE.md and AGENTS.md.**~~ **Answered 2026-11-10:** Both updated. Original question: Both still say these are out of scope with macros off. May I update both (scope lines, macros on in the chosen executables) and push?
30. ~~**P14-P17 order.**~~ **Answered 2026-11-10:** Agreed. Original question: P14 PWM, P15 SimpleMotion, P16 MyActuator, P17 MIDI (owner set the split 2026-11-10); is the order right?
31. ~~**P14-P17 fakes.**~~ **Answered 2026-11-10:** Yes. Original question: OK to add a timer / PWM-output fake and, if missing, a UART fake with real framing under `fakes/` with the usual fidelity header?
32. **P14 RC frame period.** `MotorPWM` in RC mode divides the 168 MHz timer by 85 (`timerFreq / 2000000` = 84 written into PSC, which divides by PSC + 1), so the "20 ms / 15 / 10 / 5 ms" frames are 20.24 / 15.18 / 10.12 / 5.06 ms (1.2 % long); pulse widths are exact. Is that acceptable, or a defect (charter L2: `KNOWN_ISSUE`, tolerance 0.1 %)?
33. **P14 `stopMotor`.** `stopMotor()` outputs the neutral value (RC 1500 us, centred 50 %, DIR / dual 0 %) and leaves the timer running. Intended (ESC stays armed), or should the outputs be switched off (charter: `characterization:`)?
34. **P14 RC sign.** In RC mode positive power gives a pulse SHORTER than 1500 us (`MP:73-74`). Intended? (charter: `characterization:`; a `dir` command exists to invert).
35. **P14 flash word bit 3.** `saveFlash` writes the mode in bits 0-2, `restoreFlash` reads bits 0-3 (`& 0xf`), the code comment says "1 bit free". Is bit 3 reserved (so a set bit 3 should be masked) or part of the mode (charter: `characterization:`)?
36. **P14 RC gain.** In RC mode the pulse offset is `power * 1000 / 32767` microseconds around 1500 us (`MP:73`), then clipped to 1000..2000 us: the full pulse range is reached at half scale (+-16384), +-8192 already gives 1250 / 1750 us. Is the 2x gain intended, or should full scale map to +-500 us (charter: `characterization:`)?
37. **P14 CENTERED overflow (B-PWM-4).** At the default speed (3 kHz, period 56000) the CENTERED mode overflows `int32_t` for power above about 5580 (signed overflow, the output jumps to about 100 % duty). Recorded as `KNOWN_ISSUE_UB`; for your information, no decision needed unless you want it handled differently.
38. **P15 position wrap (B-SM-2).** `MotorSimplemotion::updatePosition` adds `0xffff` per 16 bit wrap of the reported position, but the counter has period 65536 (SM: lowest 16 bits of the position feedback): every wrap loses one count (n wraps lose n counts). Defect (charter: `KNOWN_ISSUE`)?
39. **P15 voltage range (B-SM-3).** `getVoltage()` returns mV as `int16_t` (SM: 10 mV per count): above 32.767 V the value wraps negative (48 V gives -17536). Defect (charter: `KNOWN_ISSUE`), and should the command reply use more than 16 bits?
40. **P15 cpr factors.** `cpr = FBR * 4` for Hall, Resolver and Serial feedback (the source says "TODO check"); `* 16 / 64 / 256` for sincos. Intended (charter: `characterization:` for Hall / Resolver / Serial)?
41. **P15 negative values and `reg` limits.** Returned two's complement values are not sign-extended (`MS:424-425` is commented out), and `reg getat / setat` with an address above 0x3FFF overwrites the subpacket type bits. Intended, or defects (charter: `characterization:`)?
42. **P15 device address (B-SM-1).** `address(address + 1)` initialises the member from itself and ignores the `instance` argument: indeterminate address, both instances alike (charter: `KNOWN_ISSUE_UB`, tested in a poisoned buffer so it is deterministic). Defect (intended: `instance + 1`)?
43. **P15 clang build.** `MotorSimplemotion.h:175` and `:222` initialise variable length arrays (`uint8_t buf[n] = {0}`), a GCC extension that clang rejects as an error (no flag suppresses it). The firmware builds with ARM GCC, so it is only a portability note. In the test suite the clang build therefore does not compile the SimpleMotion driver or its tests (`HOSTTEST_NO_SIMPLEMOTION`, 10 cases fewer under clang). OK, or do you want clang coverage of this driver (it would need a firmware change, which is not allowed without your word)?
44. **P16 port release (B-RMD-1).** `~RmdMotorCAN` never calls `port->freePort()` although the constructor calls `takePort()` (`ODriveCAN` and `VescCAN` do): the CAN port keeps one user per driver ever created and is never stopped. Defect (charter: `KNOWN_ISSUE`)?
45. **P16 CAN id range (B-RMD-2).** `canid set` stores `(uint8_t) value` unchecked: 0 is accepted (frames to 0x140, not a motor id; SP: ID 1..32), 256 becomes 0, 33..255 are silently clamped to 32. Should it reject values outside 1..32 (charter: `KNOWN_ISSUE` for 0 and 256, `characterization:` for the clamp)?
46. **P16 max torque (B-RMD-3).** `maxtorque set` accepts up to 65535 although the header says "max 0x7fff"; `turn` converts the float result to `int16_t` (undefined above 32767) and the flash word keeps 15 bits. Should it be limited to 32767 (charter: `KNOWN_ISSUE` + `KNOWN_ISSUE_UB`)?
47. **P16 position offset (B-RMD-5).** The offset (range +-35999) is saved as `uint16_t` and restored as `int16_t`: offsets above 32767 come back negative (35000 -> -30536). Defect (charter: `KNOWN_ISSUE`)?
48. **P16 error flags and temperature (B-RMD-4, B-RMD-7).** The `ErrorStatus` bit field puts `overpower` at bit 5, `calibrationWriteErr` 6, `overspeed` 7, `overtemp` 8 and `calibrationErr` 9; the SP table has 0x0040 / 0x0080 / 0x0100 / 0x0800 / 0x1000 / 0x2000; only `asInt` is used, so there is no effect today. The temperature is stored as `uint8_t`, the SP says int8 (-10 degC reads 246). Defects (charter: `KNOWN_ISSUE`)?
49. **P16 interpolation (B-RMD-6).** In request mode the 0xA1 handler adds `speed / (1e6 * dt_us)` (5e-11 degrees at 500 dps and 10 ms, lead-corrected in T2) instead of `speed * dt` (degrees). Defect (charter: `KNOWN_ISSUE`, intended 2.5 degrees for 500 dps, 10 ms, previous position 0)?
50. **P16 design points.** (a) In the passive default the status (0x9A, the only source of the error flags) is never requested, so motor errors (over temperature, over current) are never seen; (b) the firmware never sends 0x77 (brake release), which SP 2.19 requires for a motor with a brake; (c) the 0x94 handler mixes revolutions and degrees and is never requested; (d) `canid` changes only the host side id (the motor id is set with 0x79). Intended (charter: `characterization:` / NEEDS REVIEW)?
51. **P16 `maxtorque` status (B-RMD-8, found in T2).** `command()` calls `handleGetSet` for `maxtorque` and ignores the returned status (`RmdMotorCAN.cpp:350-352`): an unsupported command type answers OK, every other RMD command answers ERR. Reachable only if the parser's flag check lets the type through. Defect (tests: `KNOWN_ISSUE`)?
52. **P16 `ErrorStatus` with two overtemperature bits (B-RMD-4).** SP 2.14 has 0x0800 (component) and 0x1000 (motor) overtemperature, the public struct has one `overtemp` flag (bit 8) and no flag for 0x4000 (encoder data error, falls into `res2`). Should the struct get two flags and the missing one (tests: both bits are separate `KNOWN_ISSUE` cases of the single flag)?
53. **P17 where `MidiMain` is tested.** `MidiMain` creates a `TMC_1` driver, so it cannot be built into `ffb_usb_tests` without the TMC4671 code. Proposal (lead L1): keep `MIDI` in `ffb_usb_tests` only and use a recording double of the TMC4671 interface (shadow header + `FakeTmcPhase`), the real driver stays in `ffb_driver_tests`. Option B: test `MidiMain` in `ffb_driver_tests` with the real TMC4671 and `FakeTmc4671`, which turns `MIDI` on in a second executable. Which one?
54. **P17 note pitch.** `play()` computes `sinf(pi * counter * f)`, so the phase oscillates at half the note frequency (A4 plays at 220 Hz). Is that intended (an octave below on purpose, e.g. because the rotor force is rectified) or a defect (it should be `2 * pi`)? Also: the A440 note table is not in the supplied documents; confirm that note 69 is 440 Hz.
55. **P17 pitch bend.** (a) The bend range is one octave (`2^(val / 8192)`); the MIDI 1.0 document leaves the sensitivity to the receiver (the usual default is two semitones). Intended? (b) A bend only affects the notes sounding at that moment, a later note ignores it (B-MIDI-2): defect (a bend is channel state) or intended?
56. **P17 mode messages and pressure.** Control changes 124-127 do not clear the notes (the MIDI document says 123-127 also act as All Notes Off but a receiver may ignore them); channel pressure is dropped without any hook. Intended?
57. **P17 cable number.** `midiRx` ignores the cable number: all cables are one instrument. Intended (the board has one MIDI port)?
58. **P17 unparsed bytes and `sysexState`.** (B-MIDI-4) Single byte packets (CIN 0xF) with data bytes are decoded as events (note off for note 0); (B-MIDI-3) `sysexState` stays set after a SysEx that ends in byte 2 or 3. Defects (`KNOWN_ISSUE`) or acceptable (no host sends them, `sysexState` is not read anywhere)?
59. **P17 `range` and `power`.** Both commands accept any 32 bit value; `range` above 32767 makes the float to `int16_t` conversion in `play` undefined (B-MIDI-5). Add a limit (defect) or document?
60. **P17 input without a handler.** (B-MIDI-6) If MIDI packets arrive while no `MidiHandler` exists, the OUT endpoint is not armed again and the MIDI input stays dead after a handler appears. Only reachable in a configuration where the MIDI interface is enumerated without `MidiMain`; defect or out of scope?
61. **P17 USB-MIDI descriptor details.** (a) Table 6-2 says wTotalLength covers the header and the jack / element descriptors, the worked example (Table B-6) and TinyUSB include the endpoint descriptors (0x41). The test uses 0x41; agree? (b) Vendor findings V86-V88 (real-time CIN, reserved CIN count, real-time inside SysEx) are reported, never fixed: nothing for you to decide unless you want them upstream.
62. ~~**P18 `ffb_dsp_tests` on 32 bit.**~~ **Answered 2026-11-11: option B.** Five test files compute reference values with `__int128`, which i386 gcc / clang lack; they are left out of 32-bit builds (option A, a portable 128-bit helper, was not chosen).

Please supply it under `Tests/Code_Coverage/shared/specs/`. Without it, HAL API expectations not derivable from RM0090 are `characterization:` and NEEDS REVIEW.

69. ~~**P12 Linux-only executable.**~~ **Answered 2026-11-11: accepted; run it in the 32-bit build.** `ffb_hal_tests` needs `-m32` and fixed-address `mmap`, so it cannot run on MinGW, macOS or the 64-bit build. Accept it behind a default-OFF option?
